{"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"05c6cb712aeaceff9a8039c474ba1723","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_function_k6w2slo712vh_1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/workspace/VULNERABILITY_FIX_SUMMARY.md","content":"# Erlang/OTP SSH Server Vulnerability Fix Summary\n\n## Vulnerability Description\n\nA critical vulnerability was discovered in the Erlang/OTP SSH server that allowed unauthenticated attackers to execute arbitrary system commands remotely by sending crafted SSH protocol messages.\n\n## Root Cause\n\nThe SSH connection handler in `ssh_connection.erl` was processing SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST messages for \"exec\", \"shell\", \"env\", and \"pty-req\" request types without verifying that the user has been authenticated.\n\nThe vulnerable message types were:\n1. **SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST with request_type = \"exec\"** - Executes arbitrary commands\n2. **SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST with request_type = \"shell\"** - Starts a shell session\n3. **SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST with request_type = \"env\"** - Sets environment variables\n4. **SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST with request_type = \"pty-req\"** - Requests a pseudo-terminal\n\n## Attack Vector\n\nAn unauthenticated attacker could send a crafted SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST message with:\n- A valid channel ID (can be guessed or discovered)\n- request_type = \"exec\"\n- Arbitrary command data in the payload\n\nThe server would process this request and execute the command without checking authentication status.\n\n## Fix Implementation\n\nThe fix adds authentication checks before processing these channel requests. The modifications were made to `/app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection.erl`:\n\n### Changes Made:\n\n1. **SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST \"exec\" handler (lines 1147-1166)**:\n   - Added check: `case SSH#ssh.authenticated of`\n   - If authenticated: Process the exec request normally\n   - If not authenticated: Return SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_FAILURE\n\n2. **SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST \"shell\" handler (lines 1135-1152)**:\n   - Added authentication check before starting shell\n   - Returns failure for unauthenticated requests\n\n3. **SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST \"pty-req\" handler (lines 1104-1140)**:\n   - Added authentication check before allocating pseudo-terminal\n   - Returns failure for unauthenticated requests\n\n4. **SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST \"env\" handler (lines 1197-1216)**:\n   - Added authentication check before setting environment variables\n   - Returns failure for unauthenticated requests\n\n### How the Fix Works:\n\nFor each vulnerable channel request type, the handler now:\n1. Checks `SSH#ssh.authenticated` field\n2. If `true`: Processes the request normally (existing behavior)\n3. If `false`: Returns SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_FAILURE response (blocks the attack)\n\nThis ensures that:\n- Unauthenticated users cannot execute commands\n- Normal SSH behavior is maintained for authenticated users\n- The server properly rejects unauthorized requests with appropriate error responses\n\n## Security Impact\n\n**Before Fix**: Unauthenticated remote code execution was possible\n**After Fix**: All channel requests require authentication, preventing unauthorized command execution\n\n## Files Modified\n\n- `/app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection.erl`\n\n## Testing Recommendations\n\n1. Verify that authenticated users can still execute commands normally\n2. Verify that unauthenticated users receive SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_FAILURE for exec/shell/pty/env requests\n3. Verify that normal SSH authentication flow still works correctly\n4. Verify that legitimate SSH clients can still connect and use the server after authentication\n\n## RFC Compliance\n\nThis fix aligns with RFC 4254 (SSH Connection Protocol) which states that channel requests should only be processed after successful authentication. The fix ensures that the server follows the proper protocol flow:\n1. Connection establishment\n2. Key exchange\n3. Authentication\n4. Channel requests (only after step 3)\n"}}],"model":"MiniMax-M2","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":144,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":752,"cache_read_input_tokens":65874},"service_tier":"standard","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d86d8de6-37c7-45d2-a0d7-19b41f9c8858","uuid":"5454178f-ebc9-4b36-8cf7-f374158a9eb6"}
